# MANGABD-009 — B2 RF-15: V-3 WRAPPER KWARG MISMATCH (GLM-5.3, 2026-10-31)

## M9-B2-RF15.0 Directive and facts

Directive (Owner live relay, zai-web IM 2026-10-31, subject "MANGABD-009 B2 RF-15: V-3 WRAPPER KWARG MISMATCH (integration seam)"): LIVE FAILURE on the Owner Run All, first full-chain 10→11→12→15 in one kernel — every region logs `render_bengali_text() got an unexpected keyword argument 'fit_w'` → rendered=0, warnings=8, blank final. Root cause stated with evidence (confirmed on bytes below): cell-10 render_page calls `render_bengali_text(..., fit_w=, fit_h=)` for quad-fit; the cells 11/12 V-3 wrapper that rebinds `render_bengali_text` does NOT accept `fit_w`/`fit_h` (it threads `fit_result` only); the battery never saw it (runs inside cell 10, wrapper undefined); chain tests called the wrapper directly; NO instrument ever ran render_page AFTER cells 11/12 in one namespace. Fix directive: (1) cells 11/12 wrappers accept `fit_w`/`fit_h` (and `fit_result`) and forward them to the wrapped function; keep forward-only discipline. (2) NEW integration battery test (cell 12 tail): exec-order simulation — after wrappers rebind, call render_page on a quad-carrying synthetic page in the SAME namespace; assert rendered>0 and no TypeError; the test must FAIL on current bytes and PASS post-fix. (3) Scope {11,12} (+ cell-12 battery); cells 6/10 byte-untouched. (4) Verify: archives byte-identical; battery green; kill-switch intact; push RF-15 commit on `mangabd-009-b2`. Flash to re-verify with a FULL-CHAIN harness (10→11→12→render_page) and add the harness pattern to the project's standard gate so this seam class never recurs. [FACT]

Base: branch `mangabd-009-b2` @ `84aa238` (Flash RF-14 re-verify, verdict RF-14 CLOSED; notebook blob `fab45a90…` == the RF-14 fixed notebook `a06085c3…` content per the Flash docs-only delta — verified pre-build by blob equality against the ref).

## M9-B2-RF15.1 Root cause (bytes)

- Cell 10 L1135-1151: the base `render_bengali_text` signature ends `fit_result=None, fit_w=None, fit_h=None` (B2 RB2-5).
- Cell 10 L1783-1798: `render_page` calls `render_bengali_text(..., text_color=…, fit_result=bf_fit, fit_w=fit_w, fit_h=fit_h)` — **the two kwargs go out for EVERY renderable region** (fit_w/fit_h are `None` unless `abs(angle) > 1.0` with a valid stored quad, but the kwarg is always passed).
- Cell 11 L296-311 and cell 12 L251-266: both V-3 wrapper signatures end at `fit_result=None`. Run All order 10→11→12 leaves the global `render_bengali_text` = the cell-12 wrapper (outermost) → cell-11 wrapper → cell-10 base.
- Call-time `TypeError` at the outermost wrapper for every region → caught by render_page's per-row `except Exception` (L1810-1816) → `log_event("Render failed […]: render_bengali_text() got an unexpected keyword argument 'fit_w'", WARN)` → `warning_count += 1`, `rendered_count` stays 0 → blank final. Exactly the Owner's symptom (rendered=0, warnings=8).
- Why nothing caught it: the cell-10 battery (Tests 1-19) executes inside cell 10 where the global is still the base function (kwargs accepted); chain tests called the wrapper directly with `fit_result` only; no instrument ever ran render_page after cells 11/12 in one namespace. **The RF-15 harness is that instrument.**
- Latency: V1m proves the wrapper bytes are identical between B1 main (`f218b35`) and the RF-15 base (`84aa238`) — the seam existed since MANGABD-003 V-3 but was harmless until B2's render_page started passing `fit_w`/`fit_h` (Phase C, RB2-5). B1's render_page called the wrapper kwarg-compatibly, which is why B1 rendered the Owner's chapter correctly.

## M9-B2-RF15.3 Fix (3 anchored patches, scope {11,12} only)

- **P1 (cell 11)** and **P2 (cell 12)**: both V-3 wrapper signatures gain `fit_w=None, fit_h=None`; the fallback arm forwards them (`…, padding, fit_result, fit_w, fit_h)` — positional, forward-only, nothing consumed or rewritten). The vertical arm is untouched and cannot receive non-None fit dims by construction: its predicate requires `abs(float(angle)) <= 1.0`, and render_page only computes fit dims when `abs(angle) > 1.0` (quad extents); the vertical renderer fits itself (`fit_font_size(text, h, w)`). Disclosed in the patch comment.
- **P3 (cell 12 tail)**: battery Test 20 — see M9-B2-RF15.4.
